{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_fa67c6939d85424da1980359","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t---\n2\tname: netscript-harness\n3\tdescription: >\n4\t  Operating model for NetScript harness runs. Use whenever the user says\n5\t  \"use harness\", references a harness run, asks about archetype/profile\n6\t  selection, run artifacts, resource aggregation, commit tracking, evaluator\n7\t  protocol, rescoping, or where a lesson/doctrine update should live.\n8\t---\n9\t\n10\t# NetScript Harness (V3) — Orchestration Skill\n11\t\n12\tThis skill coordinates harness-mode runs. The authoritative harness docs live under `.llm/harness/`;\n13\tthis skill tells you what to load and in what order.\n14\t\n15\tAgent lanes and their model bindings are configuration, not dogma — the single source of truth is\n16\t[`workflow/lane-policy.md`](../../../.llm/harness/workflow/lane-policy.md). Only two rules are hard:\n17\t**generator-session ≠ evaluator-session**, and the **slice review gate** (no implementation lane\n18\tself-certifies). Everything else is a per-run lane assignment recorded in the run dir's\n19\t`supervisor.md`/`drift.md`.\n20\t\n21\t## When to Use\n22\t\n23\t- The user says `use harness` or asks for a harnessed run.\n24\t- Selecting archetypes, scope overlays, or gate sets.\n25\t- Tracking run artifacts, commits, or drift.\n26\t- Understanding evaluator protocol (PLAN-EVAL or IMPL-EVAL).\n27\t- Deciding where a lesson or doctrine update should live.\n28\t\n29\t## When Not to Use\n30\t\n31\t- For package/plugin architecture decisions — use `netscript-doctrine`.\n32\t- For JSR readiness audits — use `jsr-audit`.\n33\t- For frontend/framework-specific questions — use `deno-fresh` or the relevant domain skill.\n34\t\n35\t## Key Concepts\n36\t\n37\t| Concept                 | Meaning                                                                                                                                                                        |\n38\t| ----------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |\n39\t| **9-phase model**       | Bootstrap → Research → Plan & Design → Plan-Gate → Implement → Gate → Evaluate → Release → Close.                                                                              |\n40\t| **PLAN-EVAL**           | Conditional pre-implementation pass for complex/decision-heavy work; hard stop when selected.                                                                                  |\n41\t| **IMPL-EVAL**           | Mandatory final pass after implementation unless the owner explicitly waives it.                                                                                               |\n42\t| **Tiered lanes**        | Agent lanes A–E with named model bindings; single source `workflow/lane-policy.md`.                                                                                            |\n43\t| **Slice review gate**   | Tier-A supervisor substantively reviews each landed slice before the sign-off commit; no lane self-certifies (A1).                                                             |\n44\t| **Supervisor identity** | Every run dir carries `supervisor.md` (model, session, host, paths, branch, baseline, lanes).                                                                                  |\n45\t| **Plan-Gate**           | Checklist (`gates/plan-gate.md`) that PLAN-EVAL enforces.                                                                                                                      |\n46\t| **Archetype**           | Package/plugin shape profile from `archetypes/ARCHETYPE-*.md`.                                                                                                                 |\n47\t| **Scope overlay**       | `SCOPE-frontend.md`, `SCOPE-service.md`, `SCOPE-docs.md`.                                                                                                                      |\n48\t| **Seed run**            | Planning-only run shape (`workflow/seed-run.md`): discovery → roadmap → owner-ratified one-shot GitHub filing. Use when the deliverable is a board (epics + issues), not code. |\n49\t| **Milestone cluster**   | Release run shape (`workflow/milestone-run.md`): Step 0 intake/cleanup/DAG → four topic orchestrators → direct-to-main leaves → canaries/stable cut.                           |\n50\t| **Run artifact**        | File in `.llm/runs/<run-id>/` that preserves state across sessions.                                                                                                            |\n51\t| **Debt**                | Recorded in `.llm/harness/debt/arch-debt.md`.                                                                                                                                  |\n52\t\n53\tFor a **supervisor run** (two or more capability-scoped phase groups), also read\n54\t`.llm/harness/workflow/supervisor.md` and `.llm/harness/workflow/escalation.md`, and track the\n55\tgroups in `phase-registry.md`.\n56\t\n57\tFor a **release milestone**, use the milestone-cluster route instead: read\n58\t`workflow/milestone-run.md` and `agent-milestone-orchestrator`. Do not apply the generic supervisor\n59\tintegration branch. Create the intake, inventory, DAG, cluster-state, and generated status\n60\tartifacts; Step 0 must validate before any implementation or evaluation lane starts.\n61\t\n62\tFor OpenHands or local-agent handoffs during a run, also read\n63\t`.llm/harness/workflow/agent-handoff.md` and `.agents/skills/openhands-handoff/SKILL.md`.\n64\t\n65\t## Workflow\n66\t\n67\tThe user may still write `profile: package`, `profile: docs`, or similar. In V3 that field is an\n68\tintent hint, not the final profile.\n69\t\n70\t| User hint                  | V3 selection                                                |\n71\t| -------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- |\n72\t| `package`                  | identify `ARCHETYPE-1` through `ARCHETYPE-6`                |\n73\t| `plugin`                   | normally `ARCHETYPE-5`, unless sibling packages also change |\n74\t| `frontend`                 | affected archetype(s) plus `SCOPE-frontend.md`              |\n75\t| `service`                  | affected archetype(s) plus `SCOPE-service.md`               |\n76\t| `docs` or `knowledge-base` | `SCOPE-docs.md` plus any described archetypes               |\n77\t\n78\t1. Read `workflow/activation.md` and `workflow/run-loop.md`.\n79\t2. If resuming, read `.llm/runs/<run-id>/context-pack.md`.\n80\t3. Identify the target surface and select archetype + overlays.\n81\t4. Read `gates/archetype-gate-matrix.md` and `gates/plan-gate.md`.\n82\t5. Scaffold run artifacts from `templates/`.\n83\t6. Produce `research.md`, then `plan.md` with locked decisions.\n84\t7. Record Design checkpoint in `worklog.md`.\n85\t8. **For complex/decision-heavy work, run PLAN-EVAL in a separate session before implementation;\n86\t   otherwise record a justified `PLAN-EVAL: N/A`.**\n87\t9. Implement one commit slice at a time; push + comment on the draft PR (the commit trail) and keep\n88\t   `worklog.md`/`context-pack.md` current after each.\n89\t10. Run gates; record results in `worklog.md`.\n90\t11. **Run IMPL-EVAL (separate session) unless the owner explicitly waives it.**\n91\t12. Close: update `context-pack.md`, `arch-debt.md`, and promote lessons if warranted.\n92\t\n93\tWhen the opening diff is docs-only, proactively apply `ci:skip-e2e` to the draft PR and also apply\n94\t`ci:skip-scaffold` when scaffold-static is not applicable. Use `ci:full` as the escape hatch when\n95\tthe docs-only change must exercise the full CI surface. Record the selection in the opening phase\n96\tcomment so the evaluator can verify that the cheap lane is intentional.\n97\t\n98\t## Human Review Surface\n99\t\n100\tKeep user-facing plans, issues, PRs, benchmark reports, and handoffs compact. Lead with the\n101\tdecision, measured evidence, total footprint, risk, and next action. Link raw evidence instead of\n102\tcopying run machinery or phase ledgers into the review surface. If the original goal is not obvious\n103\ton one screen, consolidate and simplify before asking for coordination or review.\n104\t\n105\t## Agent Delegation Contract\n106\t\n107\tLane assignments and model bindings are configuration. The tiered A–E model, the selection rules\n108\t(source slices → Tier D, batch/parallel → Tier C with committed `workflow.js`, research/doc prose →\n109\tTier B), and the named model bindings are defined once in\n110\t[`workflow/lane-policy.md`](../../../.llm/harness/workflow/lane-policy.md). Do not restate lane\n111\trouting here — defer to that file. The items below are the parts of the contract that hold as\n112\t**invariants** regardless of which lane implements:\n113\t\n114\t- **Generator ≠ evaluator (hard invariant).** The session that generates a plan or an implementation\n115\t  is never the session that evaluates it. Which model implements is configurable per run and\n116\t  recorded in `supervisor.md`/`drift.md`; the session separation is not.\n117\t- **Slice review gate (A1, hard invariant).** After any implementation lane lands a slice and its\n118\t  automated gates pass, the Tier-A supervisor substantively reviews the slice content before the\n119\t  sign-off commit — and the sign-off commit is the supervisor's, not the implementer's. This holds\n120\t  for every implementation tier (B Opus sub-agents, C Workflow-generated slices, D WSL Codex); no\n121\t  lane self-certifies. See `workflow/lane-policy.md` for the rule and `workflow/run-loop.md` for the\n122\t  step placement.\n123\t- **Evaluator route binding.** Select the evaluator route from `workflow/lane-policy.md` — the\n124\t  open-model evaluator lane for a formal PLAN-EVAL/IMPL-EVAL, or the opposite-family route for\n125\t  ordinary review — and record it in the run. The two-failure eval loop is unchanged.\n126\t- **Tier-D mobile-visibility proof.** A Tier-D (WSL Codex) implementation slice is launched only via\n127\t  skills + `.llm/tools/agentic/` (never ad-hoc `wsl.exe`), and only when the run artifacts include\n128\t  the WSL worktree path, concrete Codex thread id, daemon-managed `remote-control` proof, and the\n129\t  follow-up steering command for that same thread. Without those, record the launch as\n130\t  failed/not-attached. Claude helpers (`codex:rescue`, `codex:codex-rescue`, `codex-companion.mjs`,\n131\t  internal `general-purpose` agents) are local tool surfaces, not daemon-attached Codex threads.\n132\t- **Per-slice trackability.** Every implementation slice is independently trackable: branch/worktree\n133\t  identity, agent/thread identity, files touched, tests run, commit hash, push status, and PR\n134\t  comment/status. Each slice commits, pushes, and comments on the draft PR before the next slice is\n135\t  considered complete (the draft-PR commit list + per-slice PR comments are the commit trail).\n136\t- **Green-gate merge bar.** Merge, publish, or release gates require all relevant tests green with\n137\t  required features intact. For catalog-related work, do not delete, skip, de-catalog, or bypass\n138\t  tests unless the evaluator verdict explicitly classifies the test as stale/irrelevant and the PR\n139\t  comment records the rationale.\n140\t- **Blocked-lane handling.** If an evaluator or implementation launch path is blocked, record the\n141\t  missing launch mechanism in `worklog.md`/`drift.md`, then proceed only with an owner-authorized\n142\t  fallback lane recorded in `supervisor.md`/`drift.md`.\n143\t\n144\t## Common Pitfalls\n145\t\n146\t- **Skipping a selected Plan-Gate** — for complex/decision-heavy work, implementation before\n147\t  PLAN-EVAL `PASS` is a process failure. Small/mechanical work records `PLAN-EVAL: N/A` first.\n148\t- **Self-evaluation** — The evaluator must be a separate session. The generator does not\n149\t  self-certify.\n150\t- **Wrong evaluator surface** — the generator session may never evaluate its own output. For a\n151\t  **local-machine run**, PLAN-EVAL and IMPL-EVAL normally use a fresh native opposite-family\n152\t  session: Claude/Fable evaluates Codex-authored work and Codex/Sol evaluates Claude-authored work.\n153\t  Use the phase-bound Qwen 3.8 Flash/GLM 5.3 Flash OpenRouter preset only for a genuine third\n154\t  opinion or when the native opposite-family route is quota-blocked. If OpenRouter is then limited,\n155\t  use a fresh AGY Gemini 3.6 Flash high session on the Google subscription. OpenHands is reserved\n156\t  for explicitly cloud-driven work. For cloud PRs, repository automation owns the phase trigger:\n157\t  `openhands` plus `status:plan-eval` for PLAN-EVAL, and draft→ready for IMPL-EVAL. Supervisors\n158\t  select labels and do not duplicate the automatic dispatch with a manual evaluator. Record every\n159\t  blocked route, escalation, and requested/observed identity.\n160\t- **Self-certifying a slice** — a green automated gate is not a sign-off. The Tier-A supervisor must\n161\t  substantively review the slice before the sign-off commit, for every implementation lane\n162\t  (`workflow/lane-policy.md` invariant 2). No lane self-certifies.\n163\t- **Treating lanes as dogma** — the \"Claude coordinates / OpenHands evaluates / Codex implements\"\n164\t  fixed-lane assertion is retired. Lanes are configuration; consult `workflow/lane-policy.md` and\n165\t  honor the run's recorded lane assignments/overrides in `supervisor.md`/`drift.md`.\n166\t- **False attached-agent claims** — for Tier-D slices, a Claude `codex:*` skill/helper is not a WSL\n167\t  Codex daemon thread. Require daemon status plus thread id before claiming the user can see or\n168\t  steer the subagent from phone/Desktop.\n169\t- **Carried-in plans as ground truth** — Re-baseline against current `main` before locking the plan.\n170\t- **Monolithic commits** — Commit by slice, not by monolith. Each slice has its own gate.\n171\t- **Raw root CLI noise as a verdict** — Package-quality check/lint/fmt evidence must come from the\n172\t  scoped wrappers, not raw root `deno fmt --check` over Markdown, generated targets, or legacy\n173\t  line-ending drift (which is not a package-quality verdict source unless the plan explicitly owns\n174\t  repo-wide formatting). The wrapper invocations and verdict rules live in\n175\t  `.agents/skills/netscript-tools`.\n176\t- **Scoped wrappers as a full quality verdict** — the scoped check/lint/fmt wrappers pass code\n177\t  containing `any` and honor inline `// deno-lint-ignore no-explicit-any`, so a green wrapper run\n178\t  does NOT prove doctrine/jsr compliance. For any slice touching `packages/**` or `plugins/**`, the\n179\t  Tier-A slice review MUST additionally run `deno task quality:scan` (fails on `any`+casting and\n180\t  host-side hardcoded plugin names — the two classes that reached `main` in #745) and\n181\t  `deno task arch:check` (doctrine fitness), plus the jsr-audit skill for package/plugin waves. A\n182\t  new `// deno-lint-ignore` or `as unknown as` introduced to green a wrapper is a review-blocking\n183\t  finding, not a pass. Reviewing on wrappers alone is the exact hole that let #745 merge.\n184\t\n185\t## Run Artifacts\n186\t\n187\tRun artifacts live under `.llm/runs/<run-id>/` and use templates from `.llm/harness/templates/`.\n188\t\n189\tThey are intentionally committed cross-agent context, including the identity, worktree-path,\n190\treceipt, and resumable-state records needed to continue or audit a run. Do not strip, untrack,\n191\tredact, or block a PR merely because its scoped run directory contains those records. Secrets and\n192\ttokens are forbidden in run artifacts. Retention is owner-controlled: after a stable release the\n193\towner may select run directories for cleanup and may preserve selected runs across milestones; no\n194\tagent may perform that cleanup pre-release or infer a deletion set without the owner's instruction.\n195\t\n196\t`<run-id>` is the current branch name with `/` replaced by `-`, followed by `--<suffix>`.\n197\t\n198\t| File                | Purpose                                                                                           |\n199\t| ------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n200\t| `supervisor.md`     | supervisor identity (model, session, host, paths, branch, baseline, lanes) — written at run start |\n201\t| `research.md`       | deep findings, re-baseline of carried-in plans                                                    |\n202\t| `plan.md`           | approved scope, archetype, gates, debt implications                                               |\n203\t| `implement.md`      | generator prompt when needed                                                                      |\n204\t| `worklog.md`        | implementation evidence and gate results                                                          |\n205\t| `plan-eval.md`      | PLAN-EVAL verdict (separate session, before implementation)                                       |\n206\t| `evaluate.md`       | IMPL-EVAL verdict (separate session, after implementation)                                        |\n207\t| `context-pack.md`   | resumable summary                                                                                 |\n208\t| `drift.md`          | append-only drift log                                                                             |\n209\t| `phase-registry.md` | supervisor runs only: phase-group map + live status                                               |\n210\t\n211\tThere is no `commits.md` — the draft-PR commit list + per-slice PR comments are the commit trail.\n212\tKeep `worklog.md` + `context-pack.md` current as part of every slice. Supervisor runs additionally\n213\tkeep `phase-registry.md`, `final-pr-handoff.md`, and an `escalations/` folder; brief each group\n214\tagent with `templates/agent-briefing.md`.\n215\t\n216\tMilestone clusters additionally keep `milestone-intake.json`, `milestone-inventory.json`,\n217\t`milestone-dependency-dag.json`, `milestone-cluster-state.json`, generated `milestone-status.md`,\n218\t`cut-trace.md`, and `receipts/`. Run `harness:milestone:render` after every state transition and\n219\t`harness:milestone:validate` before dispatch or release-captain activation.\n220\t\n221\t## `.llm/runs` Path Caveat\n222\t\n223\tSome search/index tools may skip or lag on freshly written `.llm/runs/` run dirs (the same caveat\n224\tthat applied to the old `.llm/tmp/run/` location). Verify run paths with a direct filesystem listing\n225\twhen needed:\n226\t\n227\t```powershell\n228\tdir /s /b \".llm\\runs\\<id>\" 2>&1\n229\t```\n230\t\n231\t## Resource Aggregation\n232\t\n233\tWhen external docs or examples matter:\n234\t\n235\t1. check `.resources/deps-docs/`,\n236\t2. check `.llm/tmp/docs/`,\n237\t3. fetch official/primary docs with available docs tooling,\n238\t4. save useful extracts to `.llm/tmp/docs/<source>-<topic>.md`,\n239\t5. cite the extract in the run artifact.\n240\t\n241\t## Evaluator Separation\n242\t\n243\tThere are **two** separate-session evaluator passes. Both run on the evaluator lane selected from\n244\t`workflow/lane-policy.md`. The normal local route is a **native opposite-family session**:\n245\tClaude/Fable evaluates Codex-authored work and Codex/Sol evaluates Claude-authored work. OpenRouter\n246\tis an escalation only for a third opinion or when the native evaluator family is quota-blocked;\n247\tOpenHands is reserved for explicitly cloud-driven work. No lane may self-certify.\n248\t\n249\t**PLAN-EVAL** (before implementation):\n250\t\n251\t- Runs only for genuinely complex or decision-heavy work; quick fixes record `PLAN-EVAL: N/A`.\n252\t- Runs in a separate session on the evaluator lane selected from `workflow/lane-policy.md`.\n253\t- Reads `evaluator/plan-protocol.md` + `gates/plan-gate.md`.\n254\t- Reads `research.md`, `plan.md`, and the `## Design` section.\n255\t- Writes `plan-eval.md`.\n256\t- Emits `PASS` or `FAIL_PLAN`.\n257\t- Two `FAIL_PLAN` cycles, then escalate.\n258\t\n259\t**IMPL-EVAL** (final pass, after implementation):\n260\t\n261\t- Runs in a separate session on the evaluator lane selected from `workflow/lane-policy.md`.\n262\t- Generator writes `worklog.md`, `context-pack.md`, and `drift.md`.\n263\t- Evaluator reads `.llm/harness/evaluator/protocol.md`, the plan, worklog, context pack, drift, the\n264\t  draft-PR commit list + per-slice PR comments (the commit trail), selected archetype, overlays, and\n265\t  gate docs.\n266\t- Evaluator writes `evaluate.md` with `PASS`, `FAIL_FIX`, `FAIL_RESCOPE`, or `FAIL_DEBT`.\n267\t- Eval loop limit is two failures before escalation.\n268\t\n269\t## Commit Trail\n270\t\n271\tWhen a run requires commits, the draft PR's commit list + per-slice PR comments **are** the commit\n272\ttrail — there is no `commits.md`. Per slice:\n273\t\n274\t1. commit by implementation slice (message names what the slice proves, not what it contains),\n275\t2. push the branch,\n276\t3. comment on the draft PR with slice scope, commit hash, and gate/test evidence,\n277\t4. update `worklog.md` + `context-pack.md` as part of the same slice (a slice whose commit does not\n278\t   touch the run dir is incomplete),\n279\t5. continue to the next slice.\n280\t\n281\tThe draft PR is opened in the same session as the first commit, so its commit list is live and\n282\treviewable from mobile without cloning or diffing.\n283\t\n284\t## Rescoping\n285\t\n286\tRescope when the real work is materially larger than the approved plan or when the selected\n287\tarchetype is wrong. Confirm with the user before expanding scope.\n288\t\n289\tRecord rescope evidence in `drift.md` with severity `significant` or `architectural`.\n290\t\n291\t## Where Lessons Belong\n292\t\n293\t| Content type                                  | Destination                          |\n294\t| --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------ |\n295\t| Generic run mechanics                         | `.llm/harness/workflow/`             |\n296\t| Archetype-specific gates or false-done states | `.llm/harness/archetypes/`           |\n297\t| Stable repeated cross-run lessons             | `.llm/harness/lessons/`              |\n298\t| Package/plugin doctrine navigation            | `.agents/skills/netscript-doctrine/` |\n299\t| Deep domain expertise                         | a focused skill                      |\n300\t| Deferred doctrine violations                  | `.llm/harness/debt/arch-debt.md`     |\n301\t\n302\t## Quick Decision Tree\n303\t\n304\t```text\n305\tUser says \"use harness\"\n306\t  -> read workflow/activation.md and workflow/run-loop.md\n307\t  -> resuming? read context-pack.md\n308\t  -> deliverable is a board (epics/issues/milestones from a major feature, refactor, replan,\n309\t     or triage)? read workflow/seed-run.md — drafts only until owner ratification\n310\t  -> deliverable is a release milestone landed through multiple PRs? read workflow/milestone-run.md\n311\t     — Step 0 sweep/cleanup/DAG, four topic orchestrators, direct-to-main leaves\n312\t  -> deliverable is code for a single scoped change? stay on run-loop.md (a seed run here is\n313\t     ceremony)\n314\t  -> package/plugin? select ARCHETYPE-* and load netscript-doctrine\n315\t  -> frontend/service/docs? apply SCOPE-* overlay\n316\t  -> two or more phase groups? read workflow/supervisor.md + escalation.md, keep phase-registry.md\n317\t  -> read gate matrix + plan-gate.md\n318\t  -> complex/decision-heavy plan or genuine need for adversarial advice? run PLAN-EVAL (separate session); otherwise record PLAN-EVAL: N/A\n319\t  -> supervised? assign lanes per workflow/lane-policy.md (generator != evaluator session)\n320\t  -> slice landed + gates green? Tier-A supervisor reviews before the sign-off commit\n321\t  -> OpenHands/local/cloud handoff? read workflow/agent-handoff.md\n322\t  -> update run artifacts while working\n323\t  -> commit slice? push + comment on the draft PR (the commit trail); keep worklog/context-pack current\n324\t  -> discovered violation not fixed? update arch-debt.md\n325\t  -> evaluator is separate session\n326\t```\n327\t\n328\t## Reference Files\n329\t\n330\t| File                                            | Load when                                                                                     |\n331\t| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n332\t| `.llm/harness/workflow/activation.md`           | Every harness run                                                                             |\n333\t| `.llm/harness/workflow/run-loop.md`             | Every harness run                                                                             |\n334\t| `.llm/harness/workflow/lane-policy.md`          | Lane assignment + model bindings                                                              |\n335\t| `.llm/harness/workflow/doc-audit.md`            | Docs-changeset runs — opposite-family Sol audit + Fable prose polish of Claude-generated docs |\n336\t| `.llm/harness/workflow/supervisor.md`           | Multi-group supervisor runs                                                                   |\n337\t| `.llm/harness/workflow/seed-run.md`             | Planning-only board-seeding runs (discovery → roadmap → owner-ratified filing)                |\n338\t| `.llm/harness/workflow/milestone-run.md`        | Release milestone clusters from Step 0 through stable cut                                     |\n339\t| `.llm/harness/gates/plan-gate.md`               | Plan-Gate checklist    |\n341\t| `.llm/harness/evaluator/protocol.md`            | IMPL-EVAL instructions                                                                        |\n342\t| `.llm/harness/evaluator/verdict-definitions.md` | Verdict meanings                                                                              |\n343\t| `.llm/harness/gates/archetype-gate-matrix.md`   | Gate selection                                                                                |\n344\t| `.llm/harness/archetypes/README.md`             | Archetype selection [ ] `workflow/activation.md` and `workflow/run-loop.md` were read.\n351\t- [ ] Run dir has `supervisor.md` (supervisor identity + lane table) written at run start.\n352\t- [ ] Archetype and overlays netscript-harness\ndescription: >\n  Operating model for NetScript harness runs. Use whenever the user says\n  \"use harness\", references a harness run, asks about archetype/profile\n  selection, run artifacts, resource aggregation, commit tracking, evaluator\n  protocol, rescoping, or where a lesson/doctrine update should live.\n---\n\n# NetScript Harness (V3) — Orchestration Skill\n\nThis skill coordinates harness-mode runs. The authoritative harness docs live under `.llm/harness/`;\nthis skill tells you what to load and in what order.\n\nAgent lanes and their model bindings are configuration, not dogma — the single source of truth is\n[`workflow/lane-policy.md`](../../../.llm/harness/workflow/lane-policy.md). Only two rules are hard:\n**generator-session ≠ evaluator-session**, and the **slice review gate** (no implementation lane\nself-certifies). Everything else is a per-run lane assignment recorded in the run dir's\n`supervisor.md`/`drift.md`.\n\n## When to Use\n\n- The user says `use harness` or asks for a harnessed run.\n- Selecting archetypes, scope overlays, or gate sets.\n- Tracking run artifacts, commits, or drift.\n- Understanding evaluator protocol (PLAN-EVAL or IMPL-EVAL).\n- Deciding where a lesson or doctrine update should live.\n\n## When Not to Use\n\n- For package/plugin architecture decisions — use `netscript-doctrine`.\n- For JSR readiness audits — use `jsr-audit`.\n- For frontend/framework-specific questions — use `deno-fresh` or the relevant domain skill.\n\n## Key Concepts\n\n| Concept                 | Meaning                                                                                                                                                                        |\n| ----------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |\n| **9-phase model**       | Bootstrap → Research → Plan & Design → Plan-Gate → Implement → Gate → Evaluate → Release → Close.                                                                              |\n| **PLAN-EVAL**           | Conditional pre-implementation pass for complex/decision-heavy work; hard stop when selected.                                                                                  |\n| **IMPL-EVAL**           | Mandatory final pass after implementation unless the owner explicitly waives it.                                                                                               |\n| **Tiered lanes**        | Agent lanes A–E with named model bindings; single source `workflow/lane-policy.md`.                                                                                            |\n| **Slice review gate**   | Tier-A supervisor substantively reviews each landed slice before the sign-off commit; no lane self-certifies (A1).                                                             |\n| **Supervisor identity** | Every run dir carries `supervisor.md` (model, session, host, paths, branch, baseline, lanes).                                                                                  |\n| **Plan-Gate**           | Checklist (`gates/plan-gate.md`) that PLAN-EVAL enforces.                                                                                                                      |\n| **Archetype**           | Package/plugin shape profile from `archetypes/ARCHETYPE-*.md`.                                                                                                                 |\n| **Scope overlay**       | `SCOPE-frontend.md`, `SCOPE-service.md`, `SCOPE-docs.md`.                                                                                                                      |\n| **Seed run**            | Planning-only run shape (`workflow/seed-run.md`): discovery → roadmap → owner-ratified one-shot GitHub filing. Use when the deliverable is a board (epics + issues), not code. |\n| **Milestone cluster**   | Release run shape (`workflow/milestone-run.md`): Step 0 intake/cleanup/DAG → four topic orchestrators → direct-to-main leaves → canaries/stable cut.                           |\n| **Run artifact**        | File in `.llm/runs/<run-id>/` that preserves state across sessions.                                                                                                            |\n| **Debt**                | Recorded in `.llm/harness/debt/arch-debt.md`.                                                                                                                                  |\n\nFor a **supervisor run** (two or more capability-scoped phase groups), also read\n`.llm/harness/workflow/supervisor.md` and `.llm/harness/workflow/escalation.md`, and track the\ngroups in `phase-registry.md`.\n\nFor a **release milestone**, use the milestone-cluster route instead: read\n`workflow/milestone-run.md` and `agent-milestone-orchestrator`. Do not apply the generic supervisor\nintegration branch. Create the intake, inventory, DAG, cluster-state, and generated status\nartifacts; Step 0 must validate before any implementation or evaluation lane starts.\n\nFor OpenHands or local-agent handoffs during a run, also read\n`.llm/harness/workflow/agent-handoff.md` and `.agents/skills/openhands-handoff/SKILL.md`.\n\n## Workflow\n\nThe user may still write `profile: package`, `profile: docs`, or similar. In V3 that field is an\nintent hint, not the final profile.\n\n| User hint                  | V3 selection                                                |\n| -------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `package`                  | identify `ARCHETYPE-1` through `ARCHETYPE-6`                |\n| `plugin`                   | normally `ARCHETYPE-5`, unless sibling packages also change |\n| `frontend`                 | affected archetype(s) plus `SCOPE-frontend.md`              |\n| `service`                  | affected archetype(s) plus `SCOPE-service.md`               |\n| `docs` or `knowledge-base` | `SCOPE-docs.md` plus any described archetypes               |\n\n1. Read `workflow/activation.md` and `workflow/run-loop.md`.\n2. If resuming, read `.llm/runs/<run-id>/context-pack.md`.\n3. Identify the target surface and select archetype + overlays.\n4. Read `gates/archetype-gate-matrix.md` and `gates/plan-gate.md`.\n5. Scaffold run artifacts from `templates/`.\n6. Produce `research.md`, then `plan.md` with locked decisions.\n7. Record Design checkpoint in `worklog.md`.\n8. **For complex/decision-heavy work, run PLAN-EVAL in a separate session before implementation;\n   otherwise record a justified `PLAN-EVAL: N/A`.**\n9. Implement one commit slice at a time; push + comment on the draft PR (the commit trail) and keep\n   `worklog.md`/`context-pack.md` current after each.\n10. Run gates; record results in `worklog.md`.\n11. **Run IMPL-EVAL (separate session) unless the owner explicitly waives it.**\n12. Close: update `context-pack.md`, `arch-debt.md`, and promote lessons if warranted.\n\nWhen the opening diff is docs-only, proactively apply `ci:skip-e2e` to the draft PR and also apply\n`ci:skip-scaffold` when scaffold-static is not applicable. Use `ci:full` as the escape hatch when\nthe docs-only change must exercise the full CI surface. Record the selection in the opening phase\ncomment so the evaluator can verify that the cheap lane is intentional.\n\n## Human Review Surface\n\nKeep user-facing plans, issues, PRs, benchmark reports, and handoffs compact. Lead with the\ndecision, measured evidence, total footprint, risk, and next action. Link raw evidence instead of\ncopying run machinery or phase ledgers into the review surface. If the original goal is not obvious\non one screen, consolidate and simplify before asking for coordination or review.\n\n## Agent Delegation Contract\n\nLane assignments and model bindings are configuration. The tiered A–E model, the selection rules\n(source slices → Tier D, batch/parallel → Tier C with committed `workflow.js`, research/doc prose →\nTier B), and the named model bindings are defined once in\n[`workflow/lane-policy.md`](../../../.llm/harness/workflow/lane-policy.md). Do not restate lane\nrouting here — defer to that file. The items below are the parts of the contract that hold as\n**invariants** regardless of which lane implements:\n\n- **Generator ≠ evaluator (hard invariant).** The session that generates a plan or an implementation\n  is never the session that evaluates it. Which model implements is configurable per run and\n  recorded in `supervisor.md`/`drift.md`; the session separation is not.\n- **Slice review gate (A1, hard invariant).** After any implementation lane lands a slice and its\n  automated gates pass, the Tier-A supervisor substantively reviews the slice content before the\n  sign-off commit — and the sign-off commit is the supervisor's, not the implementer's. This holds\n  for every implementation tier (B Opus sub-agents, C Workflow-generated slices, D WSL Codex); no\n  lane self-certifies. See `workflow/lane-policy.md` for the rule and `workflow/run-loop.md` for the\n  step placement.\n- **Evaluator route binding.** Select the evaluator route from `workflow/lane-policy.md` — the\n  open-model evaluator lane for a formal PLAN-EVAL/IMPL-EVAL, or the opposite-family route for\n  ordinary review — and record it in the run. The two-failure eval loop is unchanged.\n- **Tier-D mobile-visibility proof.** A Tier-D (WSL Codex) implementation slice is launched only via\n  skills + `.llm/tools/agentic/` (never ad-hoc `wsl.exe`), and only when the run artifacts include\n  the WSL worktree path, concrete Codex thread id, daemon-managed `remote-control` proof, and the\n  follow-up steering command for that same thread. Without those, record the launch as\n  failed/not-attached. Claude helpers (`codex:rescue`, `codex:codex-rescue`, `codex-companion.mjs`,\n  internal `general-purpose` agents) are local tool surfaces, not daemon-attached Codex threads.\n- **Per-slice trackability.** Every implementation slice is independently trackable: branch/worktree\n  identity, agent/thread identity, files touched, tests run, commit hash, push status, and PR\n  comment/status. Each slice commits, pushes, and comments on the draft PR before the next slice is\n  considered complete (the draft-PR commit list + per-slice PR comments are the commit trail).\n- **Green-gate merge bar.** Merge, publish, or release gates require all relevant tests green with\n  required features intact. For catalog-related work, do not delete, skip, de-catalog, or bypass\n  tests unless the evaluator verdict explicitly classifies the test as stale/irrelevant and the PR\n  comment records the rationale.\n- **Blocked-lane handling.** If an evaluator or implementation launch path is blocked, record the\n  missing launch mechanism in `worklog.md`/`drift.md`, then proceed only with an owner-authorized\n  fallback lane recorded in `supervisor.md`/`drift.md`.\n\n## Common Pitfalls\n\n- **Skipping a selected Plan-Gate** — for complex/decision-heavy work, implementation before\n  PLAN-EVAL `PASS` is a process failure. Small/mechanical work records `PLAN-EVAL: N/A` first.\n- **Self-evaluation** — The evaluator must be a separate session. The generator does not\n  self-certify.\n- **Wrong evaluator surface** — the generator session may never evaluate its own output. For a\n  **local-machine run**, PLAN-EVAL and IMPL-EVAL normally use a fresh native opposite-family\n  session: Claude/Fable evaluates Codex-authored work and Codex/Sol evaluates Claude-authored work.\n  Use the phase-bound Qwen 3.8 Flash/GLM 5.3 Flash OpenRouter preset only for a genuine third\n  opinion or when the native opposite-family route is quota-blocked. If OpenRouter is then limited,\n  use a fresh AGY Gemini 3.6 Flash high session on the Google subscription. OpenHands is reserved\n  for explicitly cloud-driven work. For cloud PRs, repository automation owns the phase trigger:\n  `openhands` plus `status:plan-eval` for PLAN-EVAL, and draft→ready for IMPL-EVAL. Supervisors\n  select labels and do not duplicate the automatic dispatch with a manual evaluator. Record every\n  blocked route, escalation, and requested/observed identity.\n- **Self-certifying a slice** — a green automated gate is not a sign-off. The Tier-A supervisor must\n  substantively review the slice before the sign-off commit, for every implementation lane\n  (`workflow/lane-policy.md` invariant 2). No lane self-certifies.\n- **Treating lanes as dogma** — the \"Claude coordinates / OpenHands evaluates / Codex implements\"\n  fixed-lane assertion is retired. Lanes are configuration; consult `workflow/lane-policy.md` and\n  honor the run's recorded lane assignments/overrides in `supervisor.md`/`drift.md`.\n- **False attached-agent claims** — for Tier-D slices, a Claude `codex:*` skill/helper is not a WSL\n  Codex daemon thread. Require daemon status plus thread id before claiming the user can see or\n  steer the subagent from phone/Desktop.\n- **Carried-in plans as ground truth** — Re-baseline against current `main` before locking the plan.\n- **Monolithic commits** — Commit by slice, not by monolith. Each slice has its own gate.\n- **Raw root CLI noise as a verdict** — Package-quality check/lint/fmt evidence must come from the\n  scoped wrappers, not raw root `deno fmt --check` over Markdown, generated targets, or legacy\n  line-ending drift (which is not a package-quality verdict source unless the plan explicitly owns\n  repo-wide formatting). The wrapper invocations and verdict rules live in\n  `.agents/skills/netscript-tools`.\n- **Scoped wrappers as a full quality verdict** — the scoped check/lint/fmt wrappers pass code\n  containing `any` and honor inline `// deno-lint-ignore no-explicit-any`, so a green wrapper run\n  does NOT prove doctrine/jsr compliance. For any slice touching `packages/**` or `plugins/**`, the\n  Tier-A slice review MUST additionally run `deno task quality:scan` (fails on `any`+casting and\n  host-side hardcoded plugin names — the two classes that reached `main` in #745) and\n  `deno task arch:check` (doctrine fitness), plus the jsr-audit skill for package/plugin waves. A\n  new `// deno-lint-ignore` or `as unknown as` introduced to green a wrapper is a review-blocking\n  finding, not a pass. Reviewing on wrappers alone is the exact hole that let #745 merge.\n\n## Run Artifacts\n\nRun artifacts live under `.llm/runs/<run-id>/` and use templates from `.llm/harness/templates/`.\n\nThey are intentionally committed cross-agent context, including the identity, worktree-path,\nreceipt, and resumable-state records needed to continue or audit a run. Do not strip, untrack,\nredact, or block a PR merely because its scoped run directory contains those records. Secrets and\ntokens are forbidden in run artifacts. Retention is owner-controlled: after a stable release the\nowner may select run directories for cleanup and may preserve selected runs across milestones; no\nagent may perform that cleanup pre-release or infer a deletion set without the owner's instruction.\n\n`<run-id>` is the current branch name with `/` replaced by `-`, followed by `--<suffix>`.\n\n| File                | Purpose                                                                                           |\n| ------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `supervisor.md`     | supervisor identity (model, session, host, paths, branch, baseline, lanes) — written at run start |\n| `research.md`       | deep findings, re-baseline of carried-in plans                                                    |\n| `plan.md`           | approved scope, archetype, gates, debt implications                                               |\n| `implement.md`      | generator prompt when needed                                                                      |\n| `worklog.md`        | implementation evidence and gate results                                                          |\n| `plan-eval.md`      | PLAN-EVAL verdict (separate session, before implementation)                                       |\n| `evaluate.md`       | IMPL-EVAL verdict (separate session, after implementation)                                        |\n| `context-pack.md`   | resumable summary                                                                                 |\n| `drift.md`          | append-only drift log                                                                             |\n| `phase-registry.md` | supervisor runs only: phase-group map + live status                                               |\n\nThere is no `commits.md` — the draft-PR commit list + per-slice PR comments are the commit trail.\nKeep `worklog.md` + `context-pack.md` current as part of every slice. Supervisor runs additionally\nkeep `phase-registry.md`, `final-pr-handoff.md`, and an `escalations/` folder; brief each group\nagent with `templates/agent-briefing.md`.\n\nMilestone clusters additionally keep `milestone-intake.json`, `milestone-inventory.json`,\n`milestone-dependency-dag.json`, `milestone-cluster-state.json`, generated `milestone-status.md`,\n`cut-trace.md`, and `receipts/`. Run `harness:milestone:render` after every state transition and\n`harness:milestone:validate` before dispatch or release-captain activation.\n\n## `.llm/runs` Path Caveat\n\nSome search/index tools may skip or lag on freshly written `.llm/runs/` run dirs (the same caveat\nthat applied to the old `.llm/tmp/run/` location). Verify run paths with a direct filesystem listing\nwhen needed:\n\n```powershell\ndir /s /b \".llm\\runs\\<id>\" 2>&1\n```\n\n## Resource Aggregation\n\nWhen external docs or examples matter:\n\n1. check `.resources/deps-docs/`,\n2. check `.llm/tmp/docs/`,\n3. fetch official/primary docs with available docs tooling,\n4. save useful extracts to `.llm/tmp/docs/<source>-<topic>.md`,\n5. cite the extract in the run artifact.\n\n## Evaluator Separation\n\nThere are **two** separate-session evaluator passes. Both run on the evaluator lane selected from\n`workflow/lane-policy.md`. The normal local route is a **native opposite-family session**:\nClaude/Fable evaluates Codex-authored work and Codex/Sol evaluates Claude-authored work. OpenRouter\nis an escalation only for a third opinion or when the native evaluator family is quota-blocked;\nOpenHands is reserved for explicitly cloud-driven work. No lane may self-certify.\n\n**PLAN-EVAL** (before implementation):\n\n- Runs only for genuinely complex or decision-heavy work; quick fixes record `PLAN-EVAL: N/A`.\n- Runs in a separate session on the evaluator lane selected from `workflow/lane-policy.md`.\n- Reads `evaluator/plan-protocol.md` + `gates/plan-gate.md`.\n- Reads `research.md`, `plan.md`, and the `## Design` section.\n- Writes `plan-eval.md`.\n- Emits `PASS` or `FAIL_PLAN`.\n- Two `FAIL_PLAN` cycles, then escalate.\n\n**IMPL-EVAL** (final pass, after implementation):\n\n- Runs in a separate session on the evaluator lane selected from `workflow/lane-policy.md`.\n- Generator writes `worklog.md`, `context-pack.md`, and `drift.md`.\n- Evaluator reads `.llm/harness/evaluator/protocol.md`, the plan, worklog, context pack, drift, the\n  draft-PR commit list + per-slice PR comments (the commit trail), selected archetype, overlays, and\n  gate docs.\n- Evaluator writes `evaluate.md` with `PASS`, `FAIL_FIX`, `FAIL_RESCOPE`, or `FAIL_DEBT`.\n- Eval loop limit is two failures before escalation.\n\n## Commit Trail\n\nWhen a run requires commits, the draft PR's commit list + per-slice PR comments **are** the commit\ntrail — there is no `commits.md`. Per slice:\n\n1. commit by implementation slice (message names what the slice proves, not what it contains),\n2. push the branch,\n3. comment on the draft PR with slice scope, commit hash, and gate/test evidence,\n4. update `worklog.md` + `context-pack.md` as part of the same slice (a slice whose commit does not\n   touch the run dir is incomplete),\n5. continue to the next slice.\n\nThe draft PR is opened in the same session as the first commit, so its commit list is live and\nreviewable from mobile without cloning or diffing.\n\n## Rescoping\n\nRescope when the real work is materially larger than the approved plan or when the selected\narchetype is wrong. Confirm with the user before expanding scope.\n\nRecord rescope evidence in `drift.md` with severity `significant` or `architectural`.\n\n## Where Lessons Belong\n\n| Content type                                  | Destination                          |\n| --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------ |\n| Generic run mechanics                         | `.llm/harness/workflow/`             |\n| Archetype-specific gates or false-done states | `.llm/harness/archetypes/`           |\n| Stable repeated cross-run lessons             | `.llm/harness/lessons/`              |\n| Package/plugin doctrine navigation            | `.agents/skills/netscript-doctrine/` |\n| Deep domain expertise                         | a focused skill                      |\n| Deferred doctrine violations                  | `.llm/harness/debt/arch-debt.md`     |\n\n## Quick Decision Tree\n\n```text\nUser says \"use harness\"\n  -> read workflow/activation.md and workflow/run-loop.md\n  -> resuming? read context-pack.md\n  -> deliverable is a board (epics/issues/milestones from a major feature, refactor, replan,\n     or triage)? read workflow/seed-run.md — drafts only until owner ratification\n  -> deliverable is a release milestone landed through multiple PRs? read workflow/milestone-run.md\n     — Step 0 sweep/cleanup/DAG, four topic orchestrators, direct-to-main leaves\n  -> deliverable is code for a single scoped change? stay on run-loop.md (a seed run here is\n     ceremony)\n  -> package/plugin? select ARCHETYPE-* and load netscript-doctrine\n  -> frontend/service/docs? apply SCOPE-* overlay\n  -> two or more phase groups? read workflow/supervisor.md + escalation.md, keep phase-registry.md\n  -> read gate matrix + plan-gate.md\n  -> complex/decision-heavy plan or genuine need for adversarial advice? run PLAN-EVAL (separate session); otherwise record PLAN-EVAL: N/A\n  -> supervised? assign lanes per workflow/lane-policy.md (generator != evaluator session)\n  -> slice landed + gates green? Tier-A supervisor reviews before the sign-off commit\n  -> OpenHands/local/cloud handoff? read workflow/agent-handoff.md\n  -> update run artifacts while working\n  -> commit slice? push + comment on the draft PR (the commit trail); keep worklog/context-pack current\n  -> discovered violation not fixed? update arch-debt.md\n  -> evaluator is separate session\n```\n\n## Reference Files\n\n| File                                            | Load when                                                                                     |\n| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `.llm/harness/workflow/activation.md`           | Every harness run                                                                             |\n| `.llm/harness/workflow/run-loop.md`             | Every harness run                                                                             |\n| `.llm/harness/workflow/lane-policy.md`          | Lane assignment + model bindings                                                              |\n| `.llm/harness/workflow/doc-audit.md`            | Docs-changeset runs — opposite-family Sol audit + Fable prose polish of Claude-generated docs |\n| `.llm/harness/workflow/supervisor.md`           | Multi-group supervisor runs                                                                   |\n| `.llm/harness/workflow/seed-run.md`             | Planning-only board-seeding runs (discovery → roadmap → owner-ratified filing)                |\n| `.llm/harness/workflow/milestone-run.md`        | Release milestone clusters from Step 0 through stable cut                                     |\n| `.llm/harness/gates/plan-gate.md`               | Plan-Gate checklist                                                                           |\n| `.llm/harness/evaluator/plan-protocol.md`       | PLAN-EVAL instructions                                                                        |\n| `.llm/harness/evaluator/protocol.md`            | IMPL-EVAL instructions                                                                        |\n| `.llm/harness/evaluator/verdict-definitions.md` | Verdict meanings                                                                              |\n| `.llm/harness/gates/archetype-gate-matrix.md`   | Gate selection                                                                                |\n| `.llm/harness/archetypes/README.md`             | Archetype selection                                                                           |\n| `.llm/harness/templates/`                       | Run artifact scaffolding                                                                      |\n| `.llm/harness/debt/arch-debt.md`                | Debt registry                                                                                 |\n\n## Checklist\n\n- [ ] `workflow/activation.md` and `workflow/run-loop.md` were read.\n- [ ] Run dir has `supervisor.md` (supervisor identity + lane table) written at run start.\n- [ ] Archetype and overlays are selected and justified.\n- [ ] Lane assignments follow `workflow/lane-policy.md`, or the override is recorded in\n      `supervisor.md`/`drift.md`.\n- [ ] Plan-Gate checklist (`gates/plan-gate.md`) was reviewed.\n- [ ] PLAN-EVAL returned `PASS` before implementation when selected, or a justified `PLAN-EVAL: N/A`\n      was recorded first.\n- [ ] Any PLAN-EVAL used the recorded route, or the owner-authorized fallback was recorded.\n- [ ] Tier-D (WSL Codex) slices recorded daemon-managed proof, thread id, worktree, and steering\n      command.\n- [ ] The slice review gate was performed (Tier-A substantive review) before each sign-off commit;\n      no lane self-certified.\n- [ ] Each implementation slice was committed, pushed, and commented on the draft PR.\n- [ ] IMPL-EVAL is a separate session from the generator.\n- [ ] IMPL-EVAL used the recorded evaluator route (open model), or the blocked launch was recorded.\n","numLines":366,"startLine":1,"totalLines":366}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"call_776e4c8afe584e8380830e63","type":"tool_result","content":"---issue---\ntitle:\tworkers: preserve job payload type through definition, registry, and enqueue\nstate:\tOPEN\nauthor:\trickylabs\nlabels:\tarea:contracts, area:workers, orchestrator:fixes, priority:p1, status:impl, type:fix\ncomments:\t2\nassignees:\t\nprojects:\t\nmilestone:\t0.0.7\nissue-type:\t\nparent:\t\nsub-issues:\t\nsub-issues-completed:\t\nblocked-by:\t\nblocking:\t\nnumber:\t1455\n--\n## Summary\n\nNetScript 0.0.5 types a worker handler payload while building a job, but erases that payload type before registration and enqueue. An application cannot derive a safe `job id -> payload` map from the published job definition/registry, and a payload for one job can compile when enqueued for another.\n\n## Published 0.0.5 behavior\n\n1. `defineJob(...).payload<TPayload>()` narrows the handler payload while `JobBuilder` is being constructed.\n2. `build()` returns `JobDefinition<TId>` whose public shape retains `id`, `entrypoint`, `name`, and `topic`, but no payload type/schema.\n3. The workers service `JobTriggerInput` exposes `payload?: Record<string, unknown>`.\n4. Trigger-core `enqueueJob<TJobId, TPayload>(job, options)` infers the options payload independently because `JobDefinition<TJobId>` has no payload parameter.\n5. Generated job registries use `JobHandler<any>`, so generation cannot recover the erased payload type.\n\nThat means the following application invariant is not expressible using the published types: selecting literal job ID `embed-document` must require `EmbedDocumentPayload`, while selecting `transcribe-image` must require `TranscribeImagePayload`.\n\n## Expected\n\n- Preserve payload type/schema in a shape such as `JobDefinition<TId, TPayload>`.\n- Generate/export a literal job-ID-to-payload registry/type map.\n- Bind `triggerJob` and trigger-core `enqueueJob` payload to the selected job definition/ID.\n- Preserve the handler type in generated registration instead of `JobHandler<any>` at the application boundary.\n- Reuse the same definition for runtime validation, so producer and consumer cannot drift.\n\n## Consumer impact\n\nEIS-Chat must temporarily own a small shared job contract to restore compile-time and runtime safety around an otherwise native `workersContract` + `createServiceClient` transport. That compatibility seam will be removed when the published registry carries the payload mapping.\n\nRelated: #1451 tracks generated job registry operational metadata and handler erasure, but does not cover the job-ID-to-payload contract at trigger/enqueue call sites.\nShell cwd was reset to /home/agent/projects/netscript/worktrees/007-fixes","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"5458dd52-4a67-46ac-a724-a61805794047","uuid":"d080ad71-ad61-44ca-87f1-dc1bb606969a","timestamp":"2026-09-02T16:03:42.322Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"---issue---\ntitle:\tworkers: preserve job payload type through definition, registry, and enqueue\nstate:\tOPEN\nauthor:\trickylabs\nlabels:\tarea:contracts, area:workers, orchestrator:fixes, priority:p1, status:impl, type:fix\ncomments:\t2\nassignees:\t\nprojects:\t\nmilestone:\t0.0.7\nissue-type:\t\nparent:\t\nsub-issues:\t\nsub-issues-completed:\t\nblocked-by:\t\nblocking:\t\nnumber:\t1455\n--\n## Summary\n\nNetScript 0.0.5 types a worker handler payload while building a job, but erases that payload type before registration and enqueue. An application cannot derive a safe `job id -> payload` map from the published job definition/registry, and a payload for one job can compile when enqueued for another.\n\n## Published 0.0.5 behavior\n\n1. `defineJob(...).payload<TPayload>()` narrows the handler payload while `JobBuilder` is being constructed.\n2. `build()` returns `JobDefinition<TId>` whose public shape retains `id`, `entrypoint`, `name`, and `topic`, but no payload type/schema.\n3. The workers service `JobTriggerInput` exposes `payload?: Record<string, unknown>`.\n4. Trigger-core `enqueueJob<TJobId, TPayload>(job, options)` infers the options payload independently because `JobDefinition<TJobId>` has no payload parameter.\n5. Generated job registries use `JobHandler<any>`, so generation cannot recover the erased payload type.\n\nThat means the following application invariant is not expressible using the published types: selecting literal job ID `embed-document` must require `EmbedDocumentPayload`, while selecting `transcribe-image` must require `TranscribeImagePayload`.\n\n## Expected\n\n- Preserve payload type/schema in a shape such as `JobDefinition<TId, TPayload>`.\n- Generate/export a literal job-ID-to-payload registry/type map.\n- Bind `triggerJob` and trigger-core `enqueueJob` payload to the selected job definition/ID.\n- Preserve the handler type in generated registration instead of `JobHandler<any>` at the application boundary.\n- Reuse the same definition for runtime validation, so producer and consumer cannot drift.\n\n## Consumer impact\n\nEIS-Chat must temporarily own a small shared job contract to restore compile-time and runtime safety around an otherwise native `workersContract` + `createServiceClient` transport. That compatibility seam will be removed when the published registry carries the payload mapping.\n\nRelated: #1451 tracks generated job registry operational metadata and handler erasure, but does not cover the job-ID-to-payload contract at trigger/enqueue call sites.","stderr":"\nShell cwd was reset to /home/agent/projects/netscript/worktrees/007-fixes","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}
